Agent: Orchestrator (Z.ai Code)
Task: Full review & audit of the two FayaNMS source documents; reconcile them with the sandbox environment; produce the complete implementation roadmap and initial task breakdown; begin implementation.

Work Log:
- Read both uploaded documents end-to-end (3,475 + 3,089 lines).
- Audited workspace: Next.js 16 (App Router) + TypeScript + Tailwind 4 + full shadcn/ui set + Prisma 6/SQLite (db/custom.db via DATABASE_URL) + TanStack Query/Table + Zod + RHF + Recharts + Zustand + next-themes + next-intl. Bun runtime, port 3000, gateway `XTransformPort` for mini-services. No NestJS, no Python, no PostgreSQL/Redis/MinIO available. Only `/` is user-visible → single-route, client-side view navigation.
- Produced audit findings, adaptation decisions, roadmap and task breakdown below.

Stage Summary:
- See "PART A — AUDIT" and "PART B — ROADMAP" below. Implementation started with Task 1-a/1-b/1-c (Phase 0/1 Foundation).

# PART A — AUDIT (Documents + Environment)

| Document | Lines | Content |

| FayaNMS — Full Project Worktree & Design System Architecture | 3,475 | Design direction (Apex/Signal references), 8 UX principles, monorepo/worktree layout, full route map, design tokens (colors/typography/spacing/density/radius/shadows/icons), component system, dashboard/NOC layouts, table system, states, RTL/i18n, a11y, phases 0–9 with task codes, QA gates |
| Network Management… Project Plan | 3,089 | Business objectives, vendor tiers, adapter contract, collector architecture, inventory/credentials/backup/snapshot/diff/baseline/drift modules, change lifecycle+risk, incidents/SLA, monitoring/alerting, RBAC/audit, reporting, tech stack, DB entities, security, testing, phases 0–10, schedule, MVP definition, acceptance gates |

## A2. Consistency between the two documents (verified)

- ✅ Device status set matches: `ONLINE / OFFLINE / DEGRADED / MAINTENANCE / UNKNOWN / UNMANAGED`.
- ✅ Severity mapping matches (Critical/High/Medium/Low/Info; incidents SEV-1..4).
- ✅ Change lifecycle state machine matches (DRAFT→…→CLOSED + FAILED/ROLLBACK/REJECTED/CANCELLED/EXPIRED/ROLLBACK_FAILED/PARTIAL_SUCCESS).
- ✅ Risk scoring (0–100, LOW<21 / MEDIUM<41 / HIGH<71 / CRITICAL≥71) consistent.
- ✅ Restore-as-controlled-workflow (never one-click) consistent with HighRiskActionDialog pattern.
- ✅ Navigation taxonomy matches (Network / Configurations / Changes / Operations / Performance / Reports / Administration).
- ✅ MVP scope (Plan §90) fits the Design doc phases 1–8 — no contradiction.

## A3. Findings — conflicts & gaps (audit results)

| # | Finding | Severity | Resolution decision |
|---|---|---|---|
| F-01 | Docs prescribe monorepo (pnpm+turbo) with NestJS API, Python workers, PostgreSQL+TimescaleDB, Redis, MinIO. Sandbox mandates single Next.js 16 app, Prisma+SQLite, bun, one exposed port, API routes (no server actions). | Blocking (environment) | **ADR-01:** Keep the *architecture contracts* (adapter interface, job queue semantics, two-tier config storage, capability manifests) but implement in-process: API = `/api/v1` route handlers; queue = `JobExecution` table + in-process/mini-service worker; metrics = SQLite with rollup tables emulating Timescale aggregation/retention; object storage = AES-256-GCM-encrypted files under `storage/` with metadata in DB; Redis semantics via in-memory locks + DB. Swapping to NestJS/PG later only affects the persistence layer, not contracts. |
| F-02 | Docs define deep multi-level routes (`/network/devices/[id]/interfaces`…). Sandbox exposes only `/`. | Blocking (environment) | **ADR-02:** Single route `/` hosts an app shell with a client-side view router (Zustand store, persisted). Internal view keys mirror the doc route map 1:1 (`network.devices.detail`, tabs as sub-views). Real file routes remain possible post-export. |
| F-03 | Docs reference "Apex Dashboard" and "Signal Dashboard" templates as UI references. Neither exists in the workspace. | Minor | **ADR-03:** Treat them as descriptive references only. The design tokens/geometry specified in the doc (§15–§43, §75–§83) are complete enough to build the UI directly. |
| F-04 | Primary accent #2563EB / cyan #0891B2 is blue-family (normally restricted) — but explicitly specified by the user's design doc. | None | Use as specified: enterprise blue primary, cyan accent used sparingly (links, chart emphasis, focus). |
| F-05 | Auth: plan lists OAuth/OIDC/SSO/MFA as mandatory eventually; sandbox has NextAuth v4 available. | Medium | **ADR-04:** Phase 0/1 ships schema + permission model + `audit` hooks with a demo admin session (no login wall in preview). Full NextAuth credentials + RBAC enforcement UI scheduled in Phase 7 (Administration) with SSO-ready abstraction. Audit records this as a deliberate staging decision. |
| F-06 | Real device access (SSH/SNMP/RESTCONF to Cisco/FortiGate/Sophos/HPE) is impossible from the sandbox. | Blocking (reality) | **ADR-05:** Build a **Device Simulator + Job Worker mini-service** (bun, port 3030) implementing the adapter contract (connect/facts/config/backup/restore/apply/validate/rollback/metrics) over a simulated device fleet. All workflows (scheduled backup, drift, change execution, alerts, metrics) run end-to-end against simulated devices, so the platform is fully demonstrable; real adapters slot into the same contract later. |
| F-07 | Scheduler: docs need cron-style scheduled backups/reports. Sandbox has no system cron for app use. | Medium | **ADR-06:** Worker mini-service runs an internal scheduler loop (cron expressions parsed in-process) writing due jobs to the `JobExecution` queue. |
| F-08 | Time-series volume (docs: millions/day, Timescale/ClickHouse). SQLite demo cannot sustain that. | Medium | **ADR-07:** Write `MetricSample` (raw) + scheduled `MetricRollup` (5m/1h/1d avg/max/min/p95) with retention pruning. Dashboard reads rollups. Schema/queries designed so a Timescale swap is mechanical. |
| F-09 | No worktrees/git-flow possible (single sandbox project). | Minor | Map worktree ownership (§96) to folder ownership: `src/features/*`, `src/components/domain/*`, `src/app/api/v1/*`, `mini-services/worker`. |
| F-10 | Plan §88 estimates 5–7 engineers, 5–7 months. Sandbox is a single-agent codebase. | Context | Roadmap re-sequenced into vertical slices that each end in a demonstrable, browser-verifiable increment. |
| F-11 | Design doc requires RTL (EN/AR first-class) — next-intl is installed. | None | Phase 1 ships direction-aware tokens (logical CSS properties); i18n dictionaries (`messages/en.json`, `messages/ar.json`) land in Phase 8 with RTL regression pass. |
| F-12 | Config snapshots contain secrets; docs require encryption at rest + redacted viewer + download audit. | High (must not skip) | Implemented from Phase 2: AES-256-GCM file encryption, `sha256` integrity, `config.secrets.read` gate + `********` masking layer, `CONFIG_DOWNLOAD` audit events. |
| F-13 | Docs require TanStack Table with server-side pagination, saved views, bulk actions — heavy component. | Medium | Built once in Phase 1 as the single `<DataTable/>` (spec §43–§45); every module reuses it. |
| F-14 | "No test code" constraint in this environment vs docs' testing strategy (unit/contract/integration). | Context | Strategy kept as manual QA gates + browser verification per phase; adapter contract suite becomes a simulated checklist module (documented, not code) until the codebase leaves the sandbox. |

## A4. Risk register (top items)

1. **Single-route SPA growth** — view registry must stay typed and lazy-loaded to avoid one mega-bundle. Mitigation: dynamic imports per view.
2. **SQLite write contention** — worker + API concurrent writes. Mitigation: WAL mode, short transactions, queue table as single writer funnel.
3. **Simulated data realism drift** — configs must look vendor-authentic (Cisco/FortiGate/Sophos/HPE syntax) to make diff/drift demos meaningful. Mitigation: curated seed fixtures.
4. **Scope explosion** — docs describe an enterprise suite. Mitigation: strict MVP cut (Plan §90) with non-MVP backlog parked (Plan §91).
5. **Secrets masking gaps** — vendor-specific secret lines. Mitigation: centralized masking rule list per vendor, applied at render and export time.

# PART B — ROADMAP (sandbox-adapted, execution-ready)

Ordering principle: each phase ends with a browser-verifiable vertical slice + worklog entry. Parallelism noted per task ID.

## Phase 0/1 — Foundation (Tasks 1-a, 1-b, 1-c) — IN PROGRESS
- **1-a** Prisma core schema (all Part-C entities, SQLite-safe: no enums → string + TS constant maps), db push, realistic seed (org/sites, 24+ devices across cisco/fortinet/sophos/hpe with mixed health, interfaces, vendor-authentic config snapshots, 24h metrics + rollups, changes/steps/approvals, incidents/timelines, alerts, jobs, audit, maintenance window, baselines, drift).
- **1-b** Design foundation: token architecture in globals.css (neutral light/dark, primary #2563EB, accent #0891B2, success/warning/danger/info + subtle variants, radius 6/8/10/12, density data-attribute), status.ts single-source-of-truth maps, domain components (StatusDot, DeviceStatusBadge, SeverityBadge, ChangeStatusBadge, ChangeRiskBadge, BackupStatusBadge, DriftStatusBadge, KpiCard, PageHeader, EmptyState, ErrorState, SectionCard, TimeRangeSelect, FilterChip), Inter + JetBrains Mono fonts, ThemeProvider.
- **1-c** App shell at `/`: sidebar (groups per §16, 264px/72px collapse, badges, tooltips, mobile drawer), header (search, ⌘K command palette, alerts/notifications, theme+density toggles, user menu), sticky status footer, typed view router; Dashboard view (KPI row, availability chart, health distribution, active incidents, upcoming changes, backup compliance, recent activity); stub views with proper empty states; `/api/v1/{dashboard,devices,incidents,changes,alerts,jobs}` with Zod + envelope + TanStack Query + centralized query keys.
- **Gate G1:** `/` renders shell+dashboard with live API data in browser, dark/light + density toggles work, lint clean, no hydration errors.

## Phase 2 — Device Inventory & Simulation Core (2-a, 2-b, 2-c)
- **2-a** Devices DataTable (server pagination/sort/filter/columns/export, saved views, bulk actions) + device detail (tabs: overview/health/interfaces/config/backups/changes/incidents/alerts/logs/audit as sub-views) + sites + vendors + Add Device form (RHF+Zod, test-connection).
- **2-b** Worker mini-service v1 (bun:3030): adapter contract, generic+cisco/fortigate/sophos/hpe simulator adapters, capability manifests, job runner (retry/timeout/DLQ/locks), scheduler loop, health endpoint. Gateway wiring via `XTransformPort`.
- **2-c** Discovery job (simulated scan → candidate devices → import flow), CSV import, credential profiles UI (never display secrets).
- **Gate G2:** add/import device → test connection → backup-now job runs via worker → job center shows progress → device detail reflects state.

## Phase 3 — Configuration Management (3-a, 3-b, 3-c)
- **3-a** Backup engine end-to-end: policies (cron), scheduled/manual/pre/post-change backups, encrypted storage (AES-256-GCM), SHA-256, version history, retention pruning, backup compliance dashboard.
- **3-b** Config viewer (mono, line numbers, search, copy, wrap, secrets masking, fullscreen) + diff engine (unified/split, raw/normalized, normalization rules).
- **3-c** Baselines (approve snapshot), drift detection job + drift records + drift dashboard, restore workflow (guarded multi-step HighRiskActionDialog → change request).
- **Gate G3:** edit config on a simulated device → next poll detects drift → diff view shows normalized changes → baseline approve → restore guarded flow queues an approved change.

## Phase 4 — Change Management (4-a, 4-b)
- **4-a** Change CRUD + 8-step wizard (general/scope/implementation/validation/rollback/schedule/risk/review), risk scoring engine (0–100 table per Plan §23), templates (per-vendor), calendar with conflict highlighting.
- **4-b** Approval workflow (technical/security/manager/CAB, SoD rule requester≠approver for high risk), execution engine (pre-checks incl. "config changed since approval → BLOCK", pre-backup, apply steps, post-validation, post-backup, auto-rollback on failure), execution timeline UI, change dashboards.
- **Gate G4:** create change → approve as second user → execute → watch step timeline succeed; force failure → auto-rollback → incident correlation offered.

## Phase 5 — Operations: Alerts, Incidents, NOC (5-a, 5-b)
- **5-a** Alert rules, threshold evaluation in worker, dedup/grouping/suppression, maintenance-window suppression, alert stream UI (ack/assign/suppress/create incident), notifications center (separate from alerts per §74).
- **5-b** Incident lifecycle (SEV1-4, SLA timers, timeline with system/user/integration attribution), change↔incident correlation window search, RCA/PIR form + PDF export, incident dashboards, NOC fullscreen view.
- **Gate G5:** simulated device down → root alert only (children suppressed) → incident auto-created with SLA → acknowledge → resolve → PIR exported.

## Phase 6 — Performance & Metrics (6)
- Performance dashboards (device/interface/WAN/latency/availability/capacity), rollup retention policies UI, capacity risk list, time-range control everywhere, forecast (linear) capacity chart.
- **Gate G6:** 7d/30d views backed by rollups; retention pruning provable.

## Phase 7 — Administration, Security & Integrations (7-a, 7-b)
- **7-a** Users/roles/permissions UI (granular perms + site scoping), NextAuth credentials login + session enforcement, API clients (scoped tokens), webhooks (signed), notification channels (email/webhook), collectors registry, device drivers page, retention settings, system settings.
- **7-b** Audit explorer (filterable, before/after, correlation ID), audit hash chain, API governance (rate limit, request IDs), backup/download audit.
- **Gate G7:** RBAC demo — auditor sees read-only UI; secrets masked; export audited.

## Phase 8 — i18n, RTL, Accessibility & Polish (8-a, 8-b)
- **8-a** next-intl EN/AR dictionaries by domain, direction-aware layout pass, tech blocks stay LTR.
- **8-b** A11y pass (WCAG 2.2 AA targets: keyboard nav, focus, reduced motion, chart summaries), responsive QA matrix (375→1920, zoom), UX acceptance gates (§108–§110), final design governance doc.
- **Gate G8:** full QA matrix green; worklog final report.

## Phase 9 — Hardening & Demo Readiness (stretch)
Job center UX, saved-views persistence, report scheduler, seeded demo narrative ("guided tour" dataset), performance pass on DataTable with 1k+ rows.

## Parked (explicitly non-MVP per Plan §91)
AI troubleshooting, auto-RCA, flow analytics, full CMDB, firmware lifecycle, ZTP, predictive failure, capacity ML, natural-language changes, additional vendors (Juniper/Palo/CheckPoint/…), collector agent distribution, HA/DR topology.

Task ID: 1-b
Agent: frontend-styling-expert
Task: Design foundation — token architecture in globals.css, Inter/JetBrains Mono fonts + ThemeProvider wiring, status single-source-of-truth, domain components, preferences store.

Work Log:
- Read worklog (PART A audit + PART B roadmap + conventions) and existing globals.css/layout.tsx before editing; kept every existing shadcn variable working on the same `@theme inline` mechanism.
- globals.css token architecture:
  - Neutrals — light: bg #FAFAFA (neutral-50), surface/card white, surface-subtle #FAFAFA, border #E5E5E5/#D4D4D4 (neutral-200/300), text #0A0A0A/#525252; dark: bg #0A0A0A (neutral-950), surface/card #171717 (neutral-900), border #262626/#404040 (neutral-800/700), text #FAFAFA/#A3A3A3; new --surface/--surface-subtle. Layered dark surfaces, no absolute black.
  - Brand — --primary #2563EB (light+dark), --primary-hover #1D4ED8 light / #3B82F6 dark; --brand-accent #0891B2 light / #22D3EE dark (links, chart emphasis, focus only). shadcn's --accent hover surface deliberately stays neutral; brand accent exposed separately.
  - Status families — --success/--warning/--danger/--danger-orange/--info/--neutral each with -foreground and -subtle (subtle = color-mix translucent fill so badge backgrounds layer over any surface in both themes); exposed as --color-* so bg-success-subtle / text-success / border-success/25 etc. work.
  - Severity aliases — --severity-{critical,high,medium,low,info} (+ -subtle) -> danger / danger-orange / warning / info / neutral; single mapping reused everywhere.
  - Radius — explicit scale: --radius-sm 6px (small controls), --radius-md 8px (inputs/buttons/selects), --radius-lg 12px (dialogs/sheets/large panels), --radius-xl 10px (cards/KPI/section cards); explicit values because shadcn slots map modals->rounded-lg and cards->rounded-xl; --radius kept at 0.625rem for compatibility.
  - Density — :root[data-density="comfortable"|"compact"|"dense"] drives --density-row-h 44/36/28px, --density-control-h 36/32/28px, --density-toolbar-h 48/40/36px, --density-pad 16/12/8px, --density-cell-x 12/10/8px; font sizes never change. Named spacing utilities p-card/p-cell-x/h-row generated via --spacing-card/-cell-x/-row.
  - Shadows — --elevation-1/2/3 (subtle -> strong, e2/e3 reserved for menus/popovers/dialogs/command palette) exposed as shadow-e1/e2/e3 utilities; dark theme uses higher alphas.
  - Base layer — thin custom scrollbars (webkit + Firefox) via --scrollbar-thumb(-hover); :focus-visible accent outline with opt-out rule for shadcn components that render their own ring (ring resolves to accent via --ring); ::selection; prefers-reduced-motion support.
  - Utilities — .font-tech (mono, 13px, tabular figures, for IP/MAC/CLI/config) and .ltr-technical (direction:ltr + isolate for RTL layouts); logical-property guidance (ms-/me-/ps-/pe-/start-/end-) documented in CSS comments.
- Fonts: next/font/google Inter (--font-sans) + JetBrains_Mono (--font-mono), subsets ["latin"], display swap — Google Fonts fetch SUCCEEDED in this sandbox (compiled CSS contains "Inter", "Inter Fallback"; body classes present), so no fallback removal was needed; system font stacks remain wired as var() fallbacks in @theme inline. Metadata updated (title "FayaNMS — Network Operations Management", NMS description, icon kept). suppressHydrationWarning already on <html>; children wrapped in ThemeProvider (attribute="class", defaultTheme="system", enableSystem, disableTransitionOnChange) via new client wrapper src/components/providers/theme-provider.tsx.
- src/lib/domain/status.ts (no external deps): DEVICE_STATUS, SEVERITY, INCIDENT_SEVERITY (SEV1-SEV4), CHANGE_STATUS (16 lifecycle states, each with family: active/success/warning/danger/neutral), RISK_LEVEL, BACKUP_STATUS, BACKUP_COMPLIANCE, DRIFT_STATUS, JOB_STATUS, ALERT_STATUS, INCIDENT_STATUS; StatusBadgeConfig {key,label,token,icon,dotClass,badgeClass,iconClass}; getStatusConfig(map, key) safe lookup (trims/uppercases/normalizes hyphens+spaces, prefers the map's own UNKNOWN, falls back to neutral "Unknown", never throws, always icon+label). Icon names verified against installed lucide-react 0.525 exports.
- src/components/domain/: status-dot.tsx (pulse + aria), status-icon.tsx (name->lucide registry), status-badge.tsx (shared subtle-bg badge), device-status-badge.tsx, severity-badge.tsx, change-status-badge.tsx, change-risk-badge.tsx, backup-status-badge.tsx (+ BackupComplianceBadge), drift-status-badge.tsx, job-status-badge.tsx, kpi-card.tsx (loading skeleton matches final structure, trend arrow with semantic tone via positive flag, live status dot, truncation+title), page-header.tsx (breadcrumbs, max 1 primary action rendered last, 2-3 secondary, responsive stacking), empty-state.tsx, error-state.tsx (reason box, correlation ID in .font-tech, retry), section-card.tsx (rounded-xl card, density-aware p-card content), time-range-select.tsx (15m/1h/6h/24h/7d/30d/custom, controlled), filter-chip.tsx ("Vendor: Cisco x", logical ps-/pe- padding).
- src/stores/preferences.ts: zustand + persist ("fayanms-prefs") {density, sidebarCollapsed, setDensity, toggleSidebar}.
- Verification: `bun run lint` clean; `bunx tsc --noEmit` — zero errors under src/ (remaining output is pre-existing examples/ + skills/ noise outside src/, untouched by this task); curl / -> 200; compiled CSS spot-checked (.rounded-md 8px, .rounded-lg 12px, .rounded-xl 10px, .bg-success-subtle -> var(--success-subtle), .p-card -> var(--density-pad), .shadow-e1, .font-tech, .ltr-technical, .text-danger-orange all generated); dev.log shows clean fast recompiles, no font-fetch/compile/hydration errors. ui/*, page.tsx, prisma/**, package.json untouched; no new npm packages.

Stage Summary:

Task ID: 1-a
Agent: general-purpose
Task: Data foundation — rewrite prisma/schema.prisma (SQLite-safe, all Part-C entities), push to db, and build the idempotent realistic seed (prisma/seed.ts).

Work Log:
- Read worklog.md (PART A audit + PART B roadmap + PART C backlog) and audited existing files (schema.prisma was the Next.js starter default; package.json scripts; .env DATABASE_URL=file:/home/z/my-project/db/custom.db).
- Rewrote `prisma/schema.prisma`: 28 models (User, Role, Organization, Site, Vendor, Device, DeviceInterface, CredentialProfile, BackupPolicy, ConfigSnapshot, ConfigBaseline, DriftRecord, ChangeRequest, ChangeDevice, ChangeStep, ChangeApproval, Incident, IncidentDevice, IncidentEvent, Alert, AlertRule, MaintenanceWindow, MetricSample, MetricRollup, JobExecution, AuditEvent, ReportSchedule, Setting). Zero Prisma `enum` (allowed values documented in field comments — Task 1-b's `src/lib/domain/status.ts` maps align with them), zero Prisma `Json` (serialized-JSON String columns suffixed `*Json`), BigInt counters on DeviceInterface. Explicit onDelete: Cascade for parent-owned children (Device→interfaces/snapshots/baselines/drifts/metrics/alerts, Site→sites-of-org, ChangeRequest→steps/devices/approvals, Incident→events/devices, snapshots→baselines+drifts), SetNull for optional references (siteId/userId/changeId/jobId/ruleId/incidentId/approverId/ownerId…), Restrict for Device.vendorId and ChangeRequest.requesterId. Indexes on hot paths: deviceId+createdAt (snapshots), deviceId+metric+ts (samples), unique deviceId+metric+granularity+periodStart (rollups), status+priority (jobs), status/severity/criticality/backupCompliance filters, correlationId/createdAt (audit+jobs), plus @@unique deviceId+version, changeId+deviceId, incidentId+deviceId, changeId+level, changeId+order, deviceId+name (interfaces).
- `bun run db:push` → OK ("Your database is now in sync", Prisma client regenerated, no `@db` native-type issues).
- Created `prisma/seed.ts` (~1.9k lines; run with `bun prisma/seed.ts`; deterministic mulberry32 PRNG): wipes all tables via deleteMany in FK-safe order then rebuilds — verified idempotent (two consecutive runs → identical counts). Content: 1 org + 4 sites (HQ-Sanaa, DC-Aden, Branch-Hodeidah, Branch-Mukalla); 5 vendors; 26 devices (21 ONLINE / 2 MAINTENANCE / 1 OFFLINE / 1 DEGRADED / 1 UNMANAGED) with realistic hostnames/models/firmware (ISR4451-X, C9500-48Y4C, FortiGate 600F/601E, Sophos XGS 3300/2300, HPE 6300M/6400/6100, NX-OS N9K, 2960X aging…); 134 DeviceInterfaces with mixed admin/oper states and BigInt bps counters; 3 CredentialProfiles (vault:// secretRefs only); 2 BackupPolicies (incl. "0 2 * * *" daily).
- ConfigSnapshots: 34 across 9 devices (3-5 versions each) with vendor-authentic text — Cisco IOS XE (BGP/QoS/snmp/aaa blocks, per-version feature flags), Cisco Catalyst 9500 (VLANs/SVI/HSRP/spanning-tree), FortiOS (`config system global`/`interface`/`firewall policy`/`system ha`), Sophos SFOS CLI (zones/rules/ha blocks), HPE AOS-CX (`vlan 10/20/30/99`, `interface 1/1/24`, lag 1) — real sha256 + normalizedText + sizeBytes; PRE_CHANGE/POST_CHANGE pair on HQ-Core-RTR-01 tied to CHG-2026-00407. DRIFT STORY: HQ-Access-SW-01 baseline v3 approved (gold config incl. camera VLAN 30) → v4 introduces unauthorized `vlan 55 GUEST-TEMP` + port access, v5 changes `interface 1/1/24 description` UPLINK-CORE-01→02 → 2 OPEN DriftRecords vs v3 + INC-2026-00104 + DRIFT_DETECTED audit events. Baselines on 3 devices (HQ-Core-RTR-01 v4, HQ-WAN-FW-01 v3, HQ-Access-SW-01 v3).
- Metrics: 24 devices × CPU/MEMORY/UTILIZATION_IN/OUT × 24h — recent 4h at 5-min + older 20h at 15-min = 12,384 MetricSamples (diurnal wave, per-device bases: aging 2960X high, DEGRADED Sophos 96-99% spikes; UTIL samples linked to each device's uplink interface) + computed 1H MetricRollups (2,400 rows: avg/max/min/p95). See deviation note below for the two-tier resolution.
- Changes: 10 ChangeRequests CHG-2026-00401..410 — 2 DRAFT, 2 AWAITING_APPROVAL (PENDING approvals), 1 SCHEDULED (approved), 1 EXECUTING (CHECK/BACKUP PASSED, APPLY RUNNING, VALIDATE/ROLLBACK PENDING), 3 CLOSED-SUCCESSFUL (full PASSED steps + SKIPPED rollback), 1 ROLLBACK story (VALIDATE FAILED → auto-rollback PASSED) correlated to INC-2026-00105; 14 ChangeDevice, 30 ChangeStep, 15 ChangeApproval (unique changeId+level).
- Operations: 6 Incidents INC-2026-00101..106 (SEV1 INVESTIGATING w/ slaDueAt ≈ now+20min, SEV2 ACKNOWLEDGED, 2 SEV3, SEV3 RESOLVED + SEV2 CLOSED with rootCause/corrective/preventive) + 17 IncidentEvents (SYSTEM/USER/INTEGRATION) + 7 IncidentDevice links (one incident on 2 HA firewalls); 12 Alerts (8 ACTIVE incl. 1 CRITICAL BGP-down linked to INC-00101, 2 ACKNOWLEDGED, 1 SUPPRESSED by active maintenance window, 1 RESOLVED) + 3 AlertRules (CPU>95/5m, UTIL_IN≥90/15m, Device Unreachable via pseudo-metric AVAILABILITY<1/3m); 2 MaintenanceWindows (1 active on BR2-Access-SW-01, alert suppression narrative); 15 JobExecutions (9 SUCCEEDED/3 RUNNING/2 QUEUED/1 FAILED backup attempts=3, correlationId JOB-XXXXXX, incl. pre-change backup for CHG-00406 and queued VALIDATION step); 37 AuditEvents (DEVICE_CREATED/UPDATED, CONFIG_BACKUP incl. FAILURE, CONFIG_DOWNLOAD, CHANGE_CREATED/APPROVED/EXECUTED, BASELINE_APPROVED, DRIFT_DETECTED, INCIDENT_CREATED, ALERT_ACKNOWLEDGED, USER_LOGIN + LOGIN_FAILED) with beforeJson/afterJson and correlation IDs; 2 ReportSchedules; 6 Settings; 5 Users (admin/noc1/engineer1/auditor1/manager1, passwordHash null) + 6 Roles with permissionsJson.
- Fixed one bug found during dev: snapshots referenced CHG-2026-00407 before changes existed (FK violation) — reordered seeding so changes precede snapshots.
- Verification: `bun prisma/seed.ts` run twice — clean and idempotent (summary table TOTAL 15,185 both times); one-off bun spot-check script (deleted afterwards) confirmed: devices by status 21/2/1/1/1, drift device snapshot chain v1..v5 (MANUAL/SCHEDULED/BASELINE/SCHEDULED/CURRENT), VLAN 55 present in v5 raw text, sha256 length 64, FortiOS header authentic, changes by status {DRAFT 2, AWAITING_APPROVAL 2, SCHEDULED 1, EXECUTING 1, CLOSED 3, ROLLBACK 1}, alert mix {ACTIVE 8 incl 1 CRITICAL, ACK 2, SUPPRESSED 1, RESOLVED 1}, latest 1H CPU rollup avg/max/min/p95 sane, INC-2026-00105 ↔ CHG-2026-00410 correlation, jobs 9/3/2/1, BigInt counters populated; `bun run lint` clean. Only prisma/schema.prisma, prisma/seed.ts and worklog.md touched.

Stage Summary:
- Key artifacts: `prisma/schema.prisma` (28 SQLite-safe models; enum values documented as comments; `*Json` String columns; full index/relation coverage) and `prisma/seed.ts` (idempotent deterministic seed with vendor-authentic configs, drift narrative, full change/incident/alert/job/audit web). DB at db/custom.db is in sync and populated (15,185 rows; per-table counts printed by the seed).
- Decisions: JSON handled as serialized String columns (`payloadJson`, `tagsJson`, `permissionsJson`, `beforeJson`/`afterJson`, `recipientsJson`, `scopeJson`, `valueJson`…); enumerations as String + comment-documented values (align with 1-b's status maps); readable fixed IDs (dev-*, chg-2026-*, inc-2026-*, JOB-XXXXXX) for deterministic cross-references; MetricRollup unique key prevents double-rollup; Device.vendorId Restrict so vendor deletion can't cascade devices.
- Deviations from spec: (1) metric sampling two-tier (5-min last 4h + 15-min prior 20h) instead of flat 5-min×24h — flat would be 27.6k rows vs the ~15-20k cap; schema already supports 5M/1H/1D rollups; (2) 2 devices in MAINTENANCE (spec: 1) — one is the EXECUTING change target, the other holds the active maintenance window; (3) 2 BackupPolicies / 2 MaintenanceWindows instead of exactly 1 (1 window active, per spec); (4) 37 audit events (~30 asked).
- Next for other agents: 1-c can build dashboard KPIs from seeded status mixes (devices by status, open incidents/alerts, backup compliance, recent jobs, latest audit); Phase 3 diff/drift UI should demo HQ-Access-SW-01 v3↔v4/v5; worker mini-service (2-b) should pick up QUEUED jobs (job-010 VALIDATION, job-012 CONFIG_BACKUP) and the failed backup story on HQ-IDF-SW-01 is ready for retry logic.

Task ID: 2-b
Agent: general-purpose (code landed before agent context deadline; completed, fixed and verified by Orchestrator)
Task: Worker mini-service v1 (bun :3030) — adapter contract, simulators, job runner, scheduler + Next.js worker API.

Work Log:
- Agent delivered before deadline: mini-services/worker/{index,adapters,runner,scheduler,next-client}.ts (1096 lines, zero deps, port 3030 hardcoded, bun --hot), worker API routes src/app/api/v1/worker/{claim,progress,complete,tick,status}/route.ts.
- Architecture (binding): worker never opens SQLite; all persistence via http://localhost:3000 (backend-to-backend). Claim is atomic QUEUED→RUNNING via updateMany guard with attempts increment + scheduledAt filtering; complete(FAILED) requeues with 30s*attempts backoff until maxAttempts then dead-letters; claim enriches CONFIG_BACKUP payload with device fields (no second lookup); complete SUCCEEDED creates next-version ConfigSnapshot (demotes previous CURRENT→SUPERSEDED, sha256, sizeBytes, source from payload), updates device lastBackupAt/lastSeen, writes AuditEvent CONFIG_BACKUP with job correlationId; tick parses 5-field cron (ranges/lists/steps, Vixie dom/dow union), resolves policy scopeJson, excludes UNMANAGED+OFFLINE, 10-min per-device dedupe, cap 10/policy/tick; scheduler pokes tick every 30s; /health, /capabilities, /simulate/connect on 3030.
- Orchestrator found & fixed: next-client postJson unwrapped json.data but worker's own /simulate/connect answers flat {ok:true,...} → selfPost returned undefined → "sim.negotiated" crash on every backup. Fixed: return "data" in json ? json.data : json.
- Start & verify: worker started in background (bun --hot, nohup, logs mini-services/worker/worker.log + worker.out). E2E: seeded G1 backup-now jobs retried after backoff → SUCCEEDED (HQ-Core-SW-01 2328B cisco-ios; HQ-WAN-FW-01 2425B fortios); scheduler tick enqueued daily fleet backup once (17 jobs) then enqueued=0 (dedupe proven); job-012 (seeded payload without device ref) correctly failed "Unclaimable target" → dead-letters as designed; /health {claimed 18, completed 18, failed 0}; /api/v1/worker/status reachable:true + lastClaimAt; DB: new SCHEDULED v6 CURRENT snapshots with real sha256, previous demoted, devices lastBackupAt updated; dev.log clean.
- Note: VALIDATION/METRIC_POLL/CONFIG_APPLY/DISCOVERY seeded jobs untouched (types filter claims CONFIG_BACKUP only — DISCOVERY support is Task 2-c).

Stage Summary:
- Worker v1 operational end-to-end: claim → simulate connect (vendor-authentic banners) → generate config (cisco-ios/fortios/sfos/aos-cx/generic templates with per-run deltas) → progress posts → snapshot persistence + audit. Retry/backoff/DLQ semantics proven (job-012 dead-letter path).
- Contracts for 2-c: add DISCOVERY by (1) supporting type in claim types filter, (2) implementing runner branch that scans payload.subnets (seed job-014 payload is the shape), (3) persisting candidates via a new worker-facing endpoint or reusing complete with result shape agreed in 2-c.

Response gains { pruned, prunedDevices }; console.log once per tick when N>0.

"Unassigned" group for site-less devices, sorted best-first) with compliantPct, staleDevices (worst 10: never first then oldest, band OVERDUE|NEVER_BACKED_UP), bands {24h, 72h, note}.

Decision: NO code change — single-writer contention is transient and the job layer is the designed resilience point (same philosophy as the 2-c P2028 fix); documented instead of adding socket_timeout params that would require a dev-server restart mid-phase. Final drift state: 4 OPEN / 3 devices, lastCheckedAt fresh, 0 stuck jobs.
- dev.log: the only error line is the above P1008; no other runtime/compile errors; GET / 200.

Stage Summary:
- Phase 3 feature-complete. Artifacts: baselines + drift + restore APIs, worker DRIFT_CHECK (evaluate-in-Next architecture preserving the worker-never-opens-SQLite contract), baselines/drift views, HighRiskActionDialog domain component, config-tab baseline/restore actions.
- Semantics locked for Phase 4: restore creates AWAITING_APPROVAL (or autoApprove→SCHEDULED) change with 5 PENDING steps — the Phase 4 execution engine must claim/execute these; DRIFT_CHECK evaluate upserts ONE OPEN record per device (currentSnapshotId refreshed, detectedAt preserved); ConfigBaseline table is baseline source-of-truth (latest row per device), snapshot BASELINE status is cosmetic; manual drift queue = POST /api/v1/drift/check {deviceId?}.
- Known-accepted behaviors: worker-generated configs accumulate per-run deltas → baseline-vs-worker-current drift is EXPECTED and demos well; P1008 under 3-way concurrent drift evals self-heals via retry.

SOLUTION (proven): double-detached subshell `(setsid nohup env NODE_OPTIONS=--max-old-space-size=1280 ./node_modules/.bin/next dev -p 3000 >> dev.log 2>&1 < /dev/null &)` → reparents to PID 1, survives across calls (like the worker's bun process). Worker also cleanly restarted with the same pattern after its claim loop died in the outage window.
- Verification (all live): SoD — requester approving own HIGH change → 403 SOD_VIOLATION ✓. Failure path — CHG-2026-00418 (failAt=VALIDATE) → CHECK/BACKUP/APPLY PASS → VALIDATE:FAIL → original post-backup SKIPPED → ROLLBACK steps appended and PASSED → FAILED; byte-identical restore proven (rollback CURRENT v30 sha 7810eacf0419 == PRE_CHANGE v28 sha) ✓; incident INC-2026-00107 created+linked (SEV3 for MEDIUM risk) ✓. BLOCK path — CHG-2026-00419 approved then device backup shifted sha → CHECK:FAIL "Config changed on HQ-Access-SW-01 since approval (v35 → v36) — re-approval required", remaining steps PENDING, change FAILED, nothing applied ✓. Restore regression — CHG-2026-00420 SCHEDULED via 3-c route on shared lib ✓. Seed integrity — CHG-00401..409 statuses unchanged ✓. lint clean; tsc zero errors under src/; GET / 200.

  - GET /api/v1/incidents REWRITTEN: filters status/severity (csv), siteCode, deviceId (IncidentDevice join), q (title|number contains), source, ownerId, breached=1 (open + slaDueAt < now), sort createdAt|severity (SEV1 first — lexicographic works)|slaDueAt; meta adds counts.byStatus + counts.bySeverity (facet counts over the other filters), openCount, slaBreachedCount; rows carry device+alert counts, linked change {number,status}, owner, computed sla state per row.
  - GET /api/v1/incidents/[id] — full detail: header + site + owner + change header + devices (joined device/site) + events chron (kind/actor) + alerts + computed sla. 404 INCIDENT_NOT_FOUND.
  - POST /api/v1/incidents/[id]/[action] — ONE handler for 11 actions (transition table validates): acknowledge (NEW→ACK; acknowledgedAt first-time only; re-ack idempotent-ok 200 no dup event), assign (NEW/ACK/ASSIGNED→ASSIGNED; ownerId validated +/or ownerTeam), investigate/mitigate/monitor (any open → target, {note?}), resolve (open→RESOLVED + resolvedAt; note → USER event + appended to description as "Resolution: …"), review (RESOLVED→POST_INCIDENT_REVIEW), close (RESOLVED|PIR→CLOSED + closedAt; already-CLOSED idempotent-ok), save-pir (RESOLVED|PIR|CLOSED; 3 fields required; audit INCIDENT_PIR_SAVED), link-change/unlink-change (validates change exists; 409 when nothing linked). Guards: 404 INCIDENT_NOT_FOUND/CHANGE_NOT_FOUND/OWNER_NOT_FOUND, 409 INVALID_STATE, 400 Zod. Every action = 1 short transaction (maxWait 5s/timeout 20s) + USER IncidentEvent with actor attribution + AuditEvent (INCIDENT_ACKNOWLEDGED/ASSIGNED/INVESTIGATING/MITIGATING/MONITORING/RESOLVED/REVIEW_OPENED/CLOSED, correlationId INC-XXXXXX, beforeJson/afterJson). Race guard: updateMany where status === validated status.
  - GET /api/v1/incidents/correlate?changeId=&window= (default 60, 5–1440 clamp) — refAt = first started PASSED/RUNNING/FAILED step (executionStep) → scheduledStart → updatedAt; incidents with createdAt|resolvedAt in ±window OR linked to the change; annotated matchedOn(created|resolved)/deltaMinutes/deviceOverlap(shared device with change)/linked; sorted linked-first → overlap → nearest. Verified: chg-2026-00410 (seed ROLLBACK, refSource executionStep) → INC-2026-00105 (created+resolved, delta 15m, overlap, linked).
  - GET /api/v1/incidents/export?id= — self-contained printable HTML PIR (text/html; Content-Disposition inline; filename INC-<number>-PIR.html): header w/ severity/status/SLA outcome pill (MET/BREACHED + resolve duration vs target), description, devices, linked change, related alerts, timeline table (kind pill/actor/time), RCA/corrective/preventive boxes, sign-off lines (owner + manager/CAB), print CSS (@media print, break-inside avoid); audits INCIDENT_PIR_EXPORTED (system:report-engine, EXP-XXXXXX). No new deps.
- Backend B: GET /api/v1/incidents/stats — openBySeverity/openCount/breachedCount, MTTA + MTTR (avg minutes, 30d window, acknowledged/resolved samples counts), SLA compliance % (resolved ≤ slaDueAt / resolved w/ SLA, 30d), topSites (5, open), trend (created/day, 14d UTC buckets). Verified sane: MTTA 7.5m / MTTR 115m / compliance 100% (2 samples) on seed.
- Lifecycle API trace (live curl, INC-2026-00101): ack (idempotent, seeded ack kept) → assign from INVESTIGATING correctly 409 → investigate → mitigate → monitor → resolve{note} → review → save-pir{3 fields} → close; detail after each confirmed transitions + events (11 at end) + sla.outcome MET (resolved 23:14:53 vs due 23:16:44, SLA 1h); guards proven: resolve-after-close 409, link bogus change 404, bad owner 404, unlink 409 when none, save-pir zod min(3).
- ENV incident: dev server died (OOM reaper) mid-task → restarted with the proven double-detached subshell pattern; worker untouched on :3030.
- tsc zero errors under src/ (after fixing _lib import depths — incidents/<sub>/route.ts needs ../../_lib, [id]/[action] needs ../../../_lib; lint clean at time of this note).

Stage Summary (interim):
- Artifacts so far: src/lib/incidents/lifecycle.ts; src/app/api/v1/incidents/{route,stats,correlate,export,[id]/route,[id]/[action]/route}.ts. Endpoints: extended GET /api/v1/incidents; NEW GET /incidents/stats, /incidents/[id], /incidents/correlate, /incidents/export, POST /incidents/[id]/{acknowledge,assign,investigate,mitigate,monitor,resolve,review,close,save-pir,link-change,unlink-change}.
- Contracts: response rows embed computed `sla` (UI chips: green >50% remaining, amber ≤50%, red breached; outcome MET/BREACHED once resolved); detail `sla.dueInMs` drives live countdowns; stats powers the KPI row; correlate annotated for ranking in the change-detail "Find correlated incidents" affordance (if wired; endpoint is self-sufficient).
- REMAINING (in flight): incidents-view upgrade, incident-detail-view (ops.incident-detail wiring), NOC wallboard (ops.noc), hooks/query-keys/api-client types, browser verification, engine-incident regen drill, final reseed. See second 5-b section below when complete.

Task ID: 10-a
Agent: Z.ai Code (worker-resilience hardening agent)
Task: Phase 10-a — worker resilience hardening (claim backoff, process guards, /health observability)

Work Log:
- Read worklog (9-a/9-b/9-integration closeout) confirming the twice-documented gap: worker claim loop died during dev-server outage windows with no claim retries after sustained "Unable to connect"; recovery only via worker restart. Closed that gap with zero new dependencies (bun built-ins only) and zero files touched outside mini-services/worker/{runner,index,scheduler}.ts.
- mini-services/worker/runner.ts: replaced the fixed `setInterval(() => void claimTick(), 3000)` with a self-scheduling recursive setTimeout loop (`runClaimCycle`) whose next tick is ALWAYS scheduled in a `finally` block — no rejection (claim failure, unexpected error, log failure) can break the chain. Added `claimBackoffDelay(failures) = min(3000 * 2^failures, 300_000)`; on claim-POST failure `consecutiveClaimFailures` increments and the log line is `claim failed (consecutive=N, next retry in Xs): <err>`; on first successful claim POST after ≥1 failure a greppable `backend recovered after N consecutive claim failures` line is emitted and state resets to CLAIM_INTERVAL_MS. `claiming` re-entrancy guard kept (returns "busy"); claim types/batch/concurrency semantics unchanged. Hardened executeJob's catch: `reportFailure(...)` is now wrapped in its own try/catch logging `failed to report failure for <jobId>: <msg>` so a downed backend can never produce an unhandled rejection from the failure path. Counters extended with consecutiveClaimFailures, lastClaimAttemptAt, lastClaimOkAt, currentBackoffMs — all exposed via getCounters(); existing fields untouched.
- mini-services/worker/index.ts: added process-level guards — `unhandledRejection` logs `unhandled rejection (contained): <reason>` and `uncaughtException` logs `uncaught exception (contained): <stack>`; neither exits (acceptable for this simulator service) so /health keeps answering. /health now returns `scheduler: getSchedulerState()` alongside jobs/adapters; header docs updated.
- mini-services/worker/scheduler.ts (optional item, kept minimal/consistent): tick loop converted to the same self-scheduling pattern with its own exponential backoff (30 s → 5 min cap) and counter; /health exposes `scheduler: { consecutiveTickFailures }`; greppable `scheduler backend recovered after N consecutive tick failures` line; "scheduler tick failed (consecutive=N, next retry in Xs)" log format.

Stage Summary:
- VERIFICATION: (1) `bunx tsc --noEmit` — zero errors in mini-services/worker (only pre-existing examples/ + skills/ errors remain, outside this task's boundary); `bun run lint` clean. (2) Worker restarted cleanly with `bun --hot index.ts` (deliberate clean restart instead of relying on hot-reload to avoid any zombie setInterval chain from the pre-edit module); /health ok:true with new fields consecutiveClaimFailures=0, lastClaimAttemptAt/lastClaimOkAt (ISO), currentBackoffMs=3000, scheduler:{consecutiveTickFailures:0}, existing fields unchanged.
- OUTAGE-RECOVERY GOLDEN TEST (the exact scenario that broke in 9-a) PASSED end-to-end: killed dev server :3000 (post-kill http=000, connection refused) → worker.log shows escalating backoff `claim failed (consecutive=1, next retry in 6s)` 22:32:17Z, `consecutive=2 ... 12s` 22:32:23Z, `consecutive=3 ... 24s` 22:32:35Z, `consecutive=4 ... 48s` 22:32:59Z (+ scheduler tick failure consecutive=1/next 60s) — loop stayed alive with NO restart, unlike the pre-hardening behavior. Restarted dev server (200 after ~9 s) → recovery line `backend recovered after 4 consecutive claim failures` at 22:33:47Z — exactly the scheduled 48 s retry (22:32:59+48s), plus `scheduler backend recovered after 1 consecutive tick failures`. Real job proof: POST /api/v1/jobs {type:CONFIG_BACKUP, deviceId:dev-br1-access-sw-01} → 201 JOB-ZNWLV8 → worker claimed + `SUCCEEDED: BR1-Access-SW-01 bytes=2346 flavor=cisco-ios` in ~2 s; /health after recovery: consecutiveClaimFailures=0, lastClaimOkAt recent (22:34:08.750Z), currentBackoffMs=3000, completedByType.CONFIG_BACKUP incremented. dev.log tail: clean (no dev-side errors from the test window; worker claim POSTs 200).
- Decisions: delay math kept in a single pure helper so the failure log and the scheduler always agree; `currentBackoffMs` is always the delay of the NEXT scheduled tick; concurrency-full ticks ("busy") retry at normal cadence without touching failure counters; scheduler backoff mirrors the runner's pattern (30 s base) since the optional item was trivially safe. Gap that bit Task 9-a twice is now closed: the worker survives backend outages indefinitely (5 min retry cap) and self-recovers with greppable evidence.

Task: Phase 16 — final hardening closeout: complete the twice-documented stale-RUNNING job reaper gap; reconcile git divergence; closeout

Work Log:
- Continuity: resumed post-Phase 15 (parked list empty, roadmap 0–15 complete). Filesystem-first assessment found (a) local HEAD 3737ba1 was a twin of pushed 0cfcd83 (identical trees except runtime db files) — reconciled via `git reset origin/main` (mixed; working tree untouched); (b) the Phase 13 "no stale-RUNNING reaper" gap was PARTIALLY closed already: tick route had a reaper but its type whitelist covered only 6 of 9 claimable job types (missing CHANGE_EXECUTE, REPORT_RUN, ZTP_PROVISION).
- Key constraint discovered: worker CHANGE_JOB_TIMEOUT_MS = 600_000 (10 min, ≤40 step calls) — a naive 10-min startedAt threshold could reap a legitimately-running change at the budget edge. Design: type-agnostic 10-min threshold (every non-change job has a 30s worker budget) + 15-min threshold for CHANGE_EXECUTE; startedAt-presence guard kept.
- src/app/api/v1/worker/tick/route.ts: reaper rewritten — findMany stale RUNNING (id/type/targetId) → ZTP side-effect (ztpClaim.updateMany provisioning→failed for reaped ZTP_PROVISION targets; no device exists yet since registration happens at completion) → guarded updateMany (status RUNNING re-check) → ONE summary JOB_ORPHAN_REAPED audit row per tick (actorName system:worker-scheduler, REAP- correlationId, afterJson {count, byType, ztpClaimsFailed}) mirroring the CONFIG_RETENTION_PRUNED pattern. CHANGE_EXECUTE recovery deliberately NOT duplicated here: the change-step engine's own 5-min orphan-step reaper owns rollback-or-fail on the retry's first step call. Route header docs updated.
- Ops: dev server OOM-died mid-session again (documented decay) — restarted double-detached NODE_OPTIONS=1280; force-reset attempt corrupted db/custom.db (11ms "in sync" push created 0 tables, subsequent push errored) — recovered by restoring the committed pristine db from git with server stopped (36 tables / 30 devices / 30 jobs / 3 ZTP claims / 23 CIs = exact Phase 15 closeout state); dev + worker restarted cleanly on the restored DB.
- Verification: bunx tsc 0 errors under src/ (noise pre-existing in tool-results/skills/examples); bun run lint exit 0. Golden-path probe (tool-results/reap-probe.ts, gitignored): injected 4 synthetic orphans — ZTP_PROVISION@-11min REAPED + claim flipped failed, REPORT_RUN@-11min REAPED, CHANGE_EXECUTE@-11min SURVIVED (margin proof), CHANGE_EXECUTE@-16min REAPED — tick returned reapedOrphans=3, error text "Orphaned: no worker heartbeat (reaped by scheduler tick)", finishedAt set, JOB_ORPHAN_REAPED audit {count:3, byType ZTP/REPORT/CHANGE=1 each, ztpClaimsFailed:1}; synthetic rows cleaned; 13/13 PASS.
- Browser self-verification (agent-browser): root renders (title, no page errors, console clean excl. HMR); Job Center opens with jobs + status/type filters, detail expand/collapse works; 375px scrollWidth exactly 375; dev.log clean. Post-reseed sanity: worker claim 200, GET / 200.

- AUD-101: verifyAuditChain rewritten — walks prevHash→hash LINKS from the single genesis (not the createdAt sort), detects forks/multiple-genesis/loops/dangling links/parallel chains, and returns FULLY_VERIFIED / PARTIALLY_VERIFIED / INVALID (+ unhashed + truncated + issues) instead of an unqualified valid. Admin verify route surfaces the verdict. Live demo exposed the phantom-head residual (rolled-back tx left a dangling prevHash under concurrent job writes) — verifier caught it; scripts/rechain-audit.ts added as the demo recovery tool (documented as SQLite-demo-only; production = Phase 21 advisory locks + sequence).
- UI: session canWrite now false for viewer; lib/permissions-client.ts (useCan/useCanApproveLevel via the SAME pure matcher); change-detail-view gates Execute/Cancel/Close buttons + per-level approve/reject (disabled + tooltip naming the missing entitlement, hooks hoisted above early returns); change-approvals-view queue rows disable un-entitled levels with a tooltip.

- Golden path E2E (post-fix): admin login → queue CONFIG_BACKUP → worker claim (service JWT) → /simulate/connect (control token, 200 ssh2) → job SUCCEEDED → snapshot v7 written with encAad=v1|dev-hq-core-rtr-01|7|RUNNING|SCHEDULED → decrypt + sha-verify OK. Live 403 matrix per role (create/approve/execute/restore): operator 403/403/403/(404-has-restore), engineer 201/403/(404-has-execute)/(404-has-restore), manager 403/(404-has-approve)/403/403, auditor 403/403/403/403 — exactly the seeded policy. Browser: admin wildcard all levels enabled; manager technical+manager enabled; operator approvals disabled + no Execute button; 375px scrollWidth=375; console clean.

The change engine sends a validated PLAN { kind: APPLY|ROLLBACK, anchor, slug } (anchor [A-Za-z0-9._/:-]{1,64}, APPLY slug kebab [A-Z0-9-]{1,48}, ROLLBACK slug quote-free original ≤64 or null = remove); the worker builds commands from per-flavor templates (new change-commands.ts — the single source of what may reach a device: configure→interface→description→end→write memory / FortiOS edit-block / Junos set+commit / PAN-OS set+commit) and drives a bounded PTY CLI session.
- WORKER: ssh-transport.ts gained SSH_SESSION_FAILED + sshCliSession (netmiko-style prompt-to-prompt shell driver: initial nudge, per-command expect loop with flavor prompt/error regexes, stop-on-first-rejection, global deadline, sockets always ended; found and fixed during bring-up — ssh2 shell() takes PseudoTtyOptions as its FIRST arg, and the persona server must answer session "pty" events). New live-change.ts: LIVE_CHANGE_FLAVORS registry (5 flavors: session spec + adapter/configFlavor), parseChangePlan validation, applyLiveChangePlan; resolveLiveChangeFlavor distinguishes uncertified (FLAVOR_UNSUPPORTED) from read-only-but-not-changeable (FLAVOR_NOT_CHANGEABLE).
- PERSONA SHELLS: persona-sshd.ts extended with an interactive shell surface (per-line handle loop, persona-owned mutable prompt) + dynamic command values (config-show commands now return MUTABLE text); all five personas gained config-mode state machines whose description/comment verbs MUTATE the running config, so a post-apply fetch reflects the delta (real apply→fetch→rollback→fetch proof per flavor).
- WORKER HTTP: /live/fetch-config (real SSH config collection for BACKUP/VALIDATE) + /live/apply (plan-validated controlled change) — both under the control-token "simulate" device-control scope; failure semantics consistent: VaultError/LiveChangeError/LiveAdapterError → 400 (nothing reached the device), SshError → 200 ok:false (real transport failure). /capabilities live manifest now carries apply_config for change-registered flavors.
- CERTIFY: +6 checks per flavor (invalid plan → PLAN_INVALID with no device contact; controlled APPLY over real SSH; post-apply fetch reflects the delta; controlled ROLLBACK; post-rollback restore confirmed; /live/fetch-config without credential → 400) → 81/81 PASS, deterministic across runs (one real bug found+fixed: PAN-OS prompt regex missed the op-mode ">" prompt).
- APP: new src/lib/change/live-plan.ts (VENDOR_CONFIG_FLAVORS map, changeSlugFromTitle mirroring the worker's slug rule, extractLiveAnchor per flavor — column-0 `interfaces {` anchoring for Junos so security-zone sub-blocks can't match, extractOriginalDescription, LIVE_NO_ANCHOR sentinel, expectedDescriptionMarker). change-step engine: loadChange selects dataSource+credentialProfile (vault REFERENCES only); CHECK probes live devices over real SSH; BACKUP collects the REAL config (/live/fetch-config — failure fails the change BEFORE anything is applied, mirroring a failed pre-check); APPLY runs the plan-based controlled apply + captures the REAL post-apply config as POST_CHANGE (failAt=APPLY injected engine-side BEFORE device contact — demo control can never fault hardware); VALIDATE re-fetches and asserts the change marker in the live config (failure → rollback); ROLLBACK pushes the inverse plan (restore original description or remove; no anchor ⇒ "nothing applied, nothing to restore") and snapshots the REAL post-rollback config; new workerDevicePost classifies ok / device-failure (step fails typed) / throw (retry).
- REAL DEFECT FOUND BY THE E2E: the app's response-time rate limiter shares ONE loopback bucket with the worker's change-step/progress loops — a live change self-throttled mid-run (job FAILED on HTTP 429 after 5 PASSED steps; the limiter also returned 429 AFTER handler execution, so client-retried POSTs duplicated rows). FIX: api.ts govern() now exempts requests carrying a VERIFIED service JWT (new service-auth.ts currentRequestIsServiceAuth — signature/audience/expiry checked; header presence alone exempts nothing). Post-fix e2e: one clean run, zero 429s.
- SANDBOX E2E (in-process persona harness + the REAL worker + app APIs, driver deleted after use): sign-in → SSH credential (vault://ssh/network-admin) → LIVE_SSH device → change submitted → two-level approval (one principal per level) → execute → CHECK real-SSH probe PASSED → BACKUP "captured LIVE" PASSED → APPLY plan-based session PASSED (persona config REALLY mutated) → VALIDATE "change marker present in the live running config" PASSED → post BACKUP LIVE PASSED → change SUCCESSFUL. Proven THREE times (incl. a resumption proof: re-queued job finalized a mid-flight-interrupted change to SUCCESSFUL — the engine is resumable by construction). All temp artifacts (device, credential, 6 e2e changes, driver script) removed afterwards.

- SCHEMA: two new models — ChangeExecutionLease (PK = changeId: THE lock, one live execution per change; jobId/acquiredAt/expiresAt + jobId/expiresAt indexes) and DeviceWriteLock (deviceId @unique: exclusive per-device lock; changeId/jobId/stepId/expiresAt + indexes). Migration 20260913222625_execution_guards applied via migrate deploy; drift guard exit 0. Enforcement is deliberately DB-side (unique constraints + rowcounts), so it holds across app instances — not an in-process mutex.
- GUARD LIB (src/lib/change/execution-guard.ts, pure + dependency-free): TTL policies (EXECUTION_LEASE_TTL_MS = 4h ≫ the tick reaper's 15-min RUNNING reap; DEVICE_LOCK_TTL_MS = 15 min = 3× the engine's 5-min orphan threshold), deterministic expiry builders, isUniqueConflict (duck-typed P2002 + meta.target marker, same shape as the audit-chain conflict detector in lib/db), typed 409 sentinels (ExecutionInFlightError / StepClaimLostError → reuses the driver's existing STEP_IN_FLIGHT retry path / DeviceWriteLockedError), deviceLockRows builder, isStepClaimWon (ONLY count === 1).
- SAFE-003 (execute route): tx reordered to job INSERT → lease INSERT → step injection → audit so the lease unique violation fires BEFORE any step row or audit event exists; the loser's whole tx rolls back (its job row with it) and the route answers 409 EXECUTION_IN_FLIGHT (failWithDetail) naming the active job id + correlation. Expired-lease take-over (crash valve) before acquire. Lease RELEASE: complete route SUCCEEDED-CHANGE_EXECUTE branch + terminal-FAILED branch (requeued retries KEEP the lease — same execution), jobs/[id]/cancel CANCELLED, tick reaper's orphaned-RUNNING→FAILED — three release points, all in the existing transactions.
- SAFE-004 (change-step engine): the claim tx now does a conditional updateMany WHERE id AND status='PENDING' with a rowcount check — exactly one concurrent claimant wins; count 0 throws StepClaimLostError → 409 STEP_IN_FLIGHT, touching nothing (the winner owns the change).
- SAFE-005 (change-step engine): the same claim tx also (1) deletes OUR change's previous-step locks (single-flight + CAS guarantee no concurrent executor), (2) purges expired locks (any owner — crash valve), (3) createMany exclusive DeviceWriteLock rows for every device in scope — a conflict aborts the whole tx (claim rolls back with it: no executor ever runs a step it did not fully win) → 409 DEVICE_WRITE_LOCKED naming the holding change(s) (own id filtered). RELEASE points: after the step executor returns (terminal state), in the catch path (step back to PENDING), and inside the orphan-reap tx — plus the TTL. The engine docstring's locking paragraph rewritten to the new contract.
- TESTS: tests/audit/execution-guard.test.ts (9 pins: TTLs + determinism + no-clock-mutation, P2002 classifier incl. marker-miss/junk/no-meta fail-closed, sentinel→409 code contract, lock-row builder shape/order/TTL, CAS count semantics). Suite 167 → 176.

- PURE POLICY LIB (src/lib/change/approval-policy.ts, dependency-free): quorumRequiredFor (CAB×CRITICAL ⇒ 2, everything else 1 — the audit's "enforce distinct approvers for CRITICAL changes"); APPROVAL_VALIDITY_MS (CRITICAL 14d · HIGH 30d · MEDIUM 90d · LOW 180d, unknown tier fails TIGHT at HIGH's window — governance constants in code, not env knobs, same posture as execution-guard TTLs); approvalExpiryFor (REJECTED never carries a horizon); evaluateApprovalGate — THE single gate evaluator (per-level verdicts SATISFIED/PENDING/EXPIRED/REJECTED/UNBINDABLE/NOT_REQUIRED with DISTINCT-approver quorum counting and earliest-expiry aggregation; verdict precedence REJECTED > UNBINDABLE > EXPIRED > PENDING > SATISFIED, documented); fingerprintMismatches (quorum-counting decisions must carry the CURRENT spec fingerprint); canCastDecision (re-cast only after expiry — quorum-inflation guard); UNBINDABLE semantics: APPROVED cached rows with zero decisions (pre-POL data) AND APPROVED decisions with no verifiable horizon both refuse closed — no silent bypass.
- PURE FINGERPRINT LIB (src/lib/change/fingerprint.ts, node:crypto, server-only): approvalFingerprintFor = SHA-256 (uppercase hex, snapshot-digest convention) over a VERSIONED canonical envelope (v1): changeNumber, changeType, riskLevel, deviceIds (sorted+deduped — scope is a set), operations (order/name/type, semantically sorted), restoreSnapshotId (null vs absent distinct), schedule as ISO-UTC; stable recursive key-sort serializer; riskScore deliberately EXCLUDED (derived display value — tuning scoring factors must not churn fingerprints); invalid schedule dates throw APPROVAL_SPEC_INVALID_DATE instead of serializing "Invalid Date"; shared greppable prefix APPROVAL_FINGERPRINT_UNBINDABLE.
- SHARED GATE LOADER (src/lib/change/approval-gate.ts, server-side): loadApprovalGate(changeId, now, tx?) — one implementation feeding BOTH the decision route and the execute route (change spec + rows + decisions → fingerprint + verdict + mismatchedLevels). Type lesson recorded: the exported db is an $extends-wrapped client (audit hash-chain stamping), so its interactive-tx delegates are NOT assignable to plain Prisma.TransactionClient — the tx type is EXTRACTED from the client (Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.$transaction>[0]>[0]) rather than spelled.
- DECISIONS ROUTE (POST /changes/[id]/approvals rewritten): guards kept and tightened — permission-first (P19-C), 404s, INVALID_STATE (AWAITING_APPROVAL only), requester self-approval SoD, cross-level SoD now over ChangeApprovalDecision history (a principal is bound to the FIRST level they decided for the change's lifetime, any expiry; wildcard exempt), plus NEW: ALREADY_DECIDED only blocks terminal REJECTED levels, and canCastDecision refuses a second cast while the approver's own decision is live (409 DECISION_STILL_VALID). In ONE transaction: current fingerprint computed in-tx → ChangeApprovalDecision INSERT (fingerprint + expiry stamped) → cached mirror updated → per-decision audit (afterJson carries fingerprint + expiresAt + bindable marker) → whole-gate re-evaluation → every level's cached status re-derived → gate transitions: REJECTED ⇒ change REJECTED; SATISFIED ⇒ fingerprint-mismatch check (any quorum-counting decision disagreeing with the current spec fails the WHOLE tx closed — 409 APPROVAL_FINGERPRINT_MISMATCH, nothing recorded) then change APPROVED + approvalFingerprint stamped + CHANGE_APPROVED audit with the quorum map. Response: decision {expiresAt, fingerprint} + per-level quorum verdicts.
- EXECUTE ROUTE (POST /changes/[id]/execute): the bindable gate now runs INSIDE the SAFE-003 transaction, before job/lease/step-injection, as a discriminated result (refusal flips COMMIT; queueing is still one atomic tx): PENDING ⇒ 409 APPROVALS_PENDING (quorum-aware message); EXPIRED ⇒ 409 APPROVAL_EXPIRED + fail-closed flip (change → AWAITING_APPROVAL, approvalFingerprint cleared, non-terminal levels re-opened PENDING, CHANGE_APPROVALS_INVALIDATED audit with reason EXPIRED) — the fresh approval loop is built in; UNBINDABLE (pre-POL data) ⇒ same flip with reason REBIND_REQUIRED + 409 APPROVALS_REBIND_REQUIRED; SATISFIED ⇒ POL-002 verification (change stamp AND every counting decision must equal the recomputed fingerprint) else 409 APPROVAL_FINGERPRINT_MISMATCH + CHANGE_EXECUTE_FINGERPRINT_MISMATCH audit (result FAILURE) with expected/current/mismatchedLevels; only then SAFE-003 queueing (lease INSERT serialization point unchanged). Step injection stays AFTER the fingerprint check — the approved intent is the pre-injection spec (documented).
- PROVISIONING ×3 (wizard POST /changes submit path, PATCH SUBMIT, restore-route creation transaction): rows now stamp quorumRequired: quorumRequiredFor(level, riskLevel). CHANGE DETAIL GET: approvals payload += quorumRequired + decisions[] (decision, approverName, decidedAt, expiresAt, comment, newest first). api-client.ts types updated (ChangeDetailApprovalDecision, quorum fields, POST result shape).
- SEED: seeded APPROVED rows get bindable ChangeApprovalDecision rows (fingerprint computed through the SAME libs via a per-change DB read of devices+steps, expiresAt = approvalExpiryFor(riskLevel, decidedAt), approverName denormalized) and their changes get approvalFingerprint stamped — the demo world satisfies the new execute gate without special cases; PENDING rows stay decisionless (the live demo queue). TS lesson: the repo's TS target predates ES2020 — IPv6 math implemented in 16-bit number groups, no BigInt literals.
- DELIVERY (delivery.ts): deliverSignedPost now runs resolveAndValidateWebhookUrl BEFORE any byte is sent (refusal ⇒ FAILED outcome carrying the SSRF_BLOCKED reason — never a throw, never a network attempt) and fetches with redirect:"error" (a 3xx is a recorded outcome with a dedicated message, never a hop whose target was never validated).
- ADMISSION ×4: webhook create/PATCH and channel create/PATCH return 400 SSRF_BLOCKED with the precise reason (PATCH re-checks — rotation cannot smuggle a loopback target into a stored endpoint).
- TESTS: NEW tests/audit/ssrf.test.ts (66 pins): every blocked range class + boundary-adjacent allowed addresses (172.32.0.1, 100.128.0.1, 198.20.0.1), encoded-literal normalization, admission refusals (13 blocked URLs + userinfo + scheme), hermetic delivery checks (localhost short-circuit, .invalid NXDOMAIN ⇒ plain failure not SSRF refusal, deliverSignedPost to 127.0.0.1:9 returns the recorded SSRF outcome without hanging — proving no network attempt), redirect-policy pin. The E2E caught both before any commit. Lesson recorded: synthetic identities must never flow into User-FK columns — not even audit rows.
- FINAL DESIGN (fail-closed OPT-IN): requirePermission(req, permission, { allowApiClients }) — default REFUSES client principals with 403 API_CLIENT_HUMAN_REQUIRED (no route unknowingly receives a synthetic identity); the certified opt-in surface is POST /api/v1/alerts/[id]/acknowledge (nullable FK stays null, the audit row is the accountability record). Task ID: R30-sec-p1-005
Agent: Orchestrator (Z.ai Code)
Task: "keep going" — audit SEC tier item 4 — P1-005 (the worker "vault" is an env-var lookup wearing a vault's name → real provider-based resolver)

Work Log:
- Read the audit finding (§6 table row: `mini-services/worker/vault.ts` L39 — vault:// → FAYANMS_VAULT_* env lookup) and the P1-010/011/012 worklog entries; confirmed the SEC tier order positions P1-005 next.
- REWROTE mini-services/worker/vault.ts as a provider-based resolver: FAYANMS_VAULT_PROVIDER=env|file|exec (default env). env = the Phase 22 model byte-compatible (same vaultEnvName normalization, same CREDENTIAL_UNRESOLVED message shape, now mentioning the provider escape hatch). file = FAYANMS_VAULT_FILE JSON store, lookup order exact full ref → bare path → env-style normalized name (env→file migration is an export); POSIX-only loud warning on group/other-readable mode (deliberate fail-open on the WARNING only — Windows/Docker bind-mounts cannot always carry 0600; refusing would break legitimate deployments). exec = FAYANMS_VAULT_EXEC shell-free argv template, every %s → the raw reference (appended when absent), trimmed stdout is the secret, deadline FAYANMS_VAULT_EXEC_TIMEOUT_MS (default 5000, clamp 100–60000).
- Design decision RECORDED in code and docs: references NEVER encode a provider — per-ref prefixes (vault://file/…) were REJECTED because the first path segment would be ambiguous against real vault paths; the backend is deployment-level so backend rotation never silently re-interprets an existing reference. New typed codes: VAULT_PROVIDER_INVALID (unknown FAYANMS_VAULT_PROVIDER), VAULT_PROVIDER_MISCONFIGURED (missing/malformed provider config) — consumption is instanceof+code interpolation, fully backward-compatible.
- RUNTIME TRUTH DISCOVERED AND FIXED: Bun 1.3.14 silently IGNORES node:child_process spawnSync's `timeout` option (and Bun.spawnSync's `timeout`) — verified empirically (sleep 10 with timeout 100 ran the full 10 s). A synchronous deadline would have been a lie on this runtime, so the exec provider is ASYNC: manual deadline (setTimeout → child SIGTERM, SIGKILL escalation after 1 s), stdout/stderr capped accumulation (1 MiB/64 KiB), exit-code + sanitized stderr-tail (≤200 chars) in messages, stdout (the secret) NEVER in messages. resolveVaultSecret became Promise-returning; `await` added at every call site: adapter-router.resolveAdapter (now async, Promise<DeviceAdapter>), runner.ts, index.ts (3 sites), certify.ts (3 sites incl. the expectError-awaited callback), tests/audit/ssh-hostkey-guard.test.ts (5 SAFE-001 pins converted to async awaits — policies unchanged, all still pass).
- TESTS: NEW tests/audit/vault-providers.test.ts (21 pins): grammar + env compat (normalization, CREDENTIAL_REF_INVALID for non-vault/empty paths, missing entry names the env var), provider validation fail-tight, file provider (full-ref/bare-path/env-name lookup, mis-shape taxonomy VAULT_PROVIDER_MISCONFIGURED, empty values never resolve, permissive-mode warning still resolves), exec provider (argv substitution %s in-token/multi/append, echo round-trip, exit 1 → "exited 1", empty stdout, timeout kill of a real hanging script, missing template, nonexistent binary), cross-provider grammar stability (same ref resolves through all three) and secret-never-in-messages.
- SANDBOX E2E over the live worker HTTP plane (5/5): (A) missing entry → 400 CREDENTIAL_UNRESOLVED naming FAYANMS_VAULT_SSH_E2E_ABSENT; (B) with the operator var set ON THE WORKER env (restarted worker per runbook T6 — first run correctly proved the env provider reads the WORKER env, not the caller's) → vault resolves through the async path and the failure correctly moves downstream to the SSH transport (SSH_UNREACHABLE, ECONNREFUSED port 1); (D) the resolved secret value never appears in any response.
- GATES: lint 0 · tsc 0 · bun test 374/374 (2,264 expects; suite 353 → 374) · drift guard exit 0 (no schema change) · build:gate PASS (foreground re-run after a silent background OOM-death; dev server unaffected) · worker /health OK after hot reload and after restart.
- Docs synced: README (full P1-005 LANDED paragraph incl. the Bun spawnSync timeout discovery and the async-resolution rationale), deploy doc security note 16 (provider selection + compose-shape guidance + exec deadline behavior), .env.example (provider/file/exec/timeout variables with migration examples).

Stage Summary:
- P1-005 LANDED AND E2E-PROVEN: the worker vault is no longer a misnomer — it is a real provider resolver with three backends behind an unchanged reference grammar, fail-closed on every misconfiguration and unresolved reference, with a genuine (not nominal) execution deadline on the exec path, and the secret-value-never-logged guarantee pinned in tests and proven over live HTTP. Existing Phase 22 deployments keep working unchanged (env default); migrating to a file or a real vault CLI is now a documented, one-variable operation.
- Honest posture: the file provider's permissive-mode warning is deliberately fail-open on the warning only (documented, Windows bind-mount rationale); exec stderr tails can surface in worker error messages (operator controls the command; stdout/secret never surfaces); CI remains infra-blocked as recorded in the R29 addendum.
- Next executable steps: P1-007 (asymmetric service identity — HS256 shared-secret JWT trust), CERT-006 (Sophos SFOS WebAPI transport).
- Artifacts: this commit (mini-services/worker/vault.ts rewrite; awaited call sites in adapter-router/runner/index/certify; tests/audit/vault-providers.test.ts; tests/audit/ssh-hostkey-guard.test.ts async conversions; README; deploy doc note 16; .env.example).

Task ID: R31-sec-p1-007
Agent: Orchestrator (Z.ai Code)
Task: "keep going" — audit SEC tier item 5 — P1-007 (symmetric service JWT trust: HS256 shared-secret only; holder of the secret can mint any token → asymmetric service identity)

Work Log:
- Read the audit finding and mapped the FULL trust chain: 3 verifiers (Next route handlers via authenticateServiceRequest, src/proxy.ts SAFE-002 machine-plane exemption, worker control-auth for /simulate/* + /capabilities) and 2 minters (Next mintServiceToken for Next→worker calls, worker service-token for worker→Next job-engine claims).
- EXTENDED the dependency-free core (src/lib/auth/service-jwt.ts — still node:crypto-only so the proxy graph stays lean): alg "EdDSA" (Ed25519) verified via crypto.verify(null, …) against FAYANMS_SERVICE_PUBLIC_KEYS (SPKI DER base64, comma-separated rotation list; PEM tolerated with escaped newlines). Trust policy is per-alg and fail-tight: EdDSA requires configured public keys, HS256 requires configured secrets; presenting an alg whose plane is unconfigured → SERVICE_ALG_REJECTED (never a silent fallthrough); a MALFORMED configured key → SERVICE_KEYS_MISCONFIGURED (a silently dropped rotation entry would fake trust). Parsed keys cached by raw env value (rotation via env change + cache invalidation semantics preserved in tests).
- MINTING PREFERENCE (both minters): FAYANMS_SERVICE_PRIVATE_KEY (PKCS8 PEM) present → Ed25519 tokens immediately; else legacy HS256. Next side in service-auth.ts; worker side in service-token.ts (worker reads process.env then repo .env, dotenv-quote and escaped-newline tolerant, mirroring the existing secret reader) + resetServiceTokenCache() for rotation-without-restart.
- WORKER VERIFIER (mini-services/worker/control-auth.ts, still zero-dependency): verifies EdDSA control tokens against FAYANMS_SERVICE_PUBLIC_KEYS — the CONTROL identity's public key, so the worker can never mint control-plane tokens. HS256 remains while the shared secret exists. New codes WORKER_ALG_REJECTED / WORKER_KEYS_MISCONFIGURED; audience/expiry/iat/issuer/scope pins unchanged and now apply to both algs.
- DESIGN: flag-free two-phase rotation. Phase 1 — deploy public keys to verifiers + private keys to minters (both algs accepted; minters prefer EdDSA). Phase 2 — remove FAYANMS_SERVICE_SECRET(S) everywhere; HS256 becomes structurally impossible. Recorded design decision: the recommended topology is TWO keypairs (control + worker), each side holding only the other's public half. Keygen: scripts/generate-service-keys.ts (+ package.json keys:service) prints a ready-to-paste env block with the topology comment.
- TESTS: NEW tests/auth/service-identity.test.ts (24 pins): minting preference (EdDSA wins over secret; HS256 explicit fallback; no-plane mint refuses), verifier trust policy (public-keys-only authentication; Phase-2 endpoint refuses HS256 with "symmetric plane is retired"; EdDSA without public keys refused; neither plane → SERVICE_UNCONFIGURED; foreign keypair → SERVICE_TOKEN_INVALID; rotation overlap of two keys; tamper; alg confusion none/HS384/RS256 → malformed; malformed key material → fail-tight; expired/foreign-issuer/wrong-audience; scope enforcement on the EdDSA path), worker plane (control EdDSA verify with only the control public key; legacy HS256 coexistence; WORKER_ALG_REJECTED; WORKER_KEYS_MISCONFIGURED; issuer/scope/expiry pins), and the FULL two-plane loop (worker-minted EdDSA verifies on the Next side against the worker public key only; raw Ed25519 signature round-trip). Suite 374 → 398 (2,325 expects).
- SANDBOX E2E over the live two-process configuration (5/5): generated two real keypairs, restarted BOTH processes on the asymmetric plane (env: private key + the peer's public key + both issuers) — (1) admin NextAuth login works; (2) devices list readable; (3) POST /api/v1/devices/test-connection → reachable:true latency 877ms — the REAL Next minter produced an EdDSA token that the worker verified with only the control public key; (4) no auth-refusal artifact anywhere; (5) worker→Next job claims healthy with ZERO failures — the worker minted EdDSA and Next verified with only the worker public key. The sandbox was then RESTORED to the documented default (shared-secret config; both processes healthy, claim failures 0, app 200).
- RUNTIME NOTES (honest): Bun's createPublicKey(Buffer) shorthand misreads DER as PEM (NO_START_LINE) — the explicit {key,format,type} object form is the portable one (pinned in tests). The Next verifier's issuer allowlist defaults to the worker identity only; two-plane deployments set FAYANMS_SERVICE_ISSUERS=fayanms:control,fayanms:worker (documented in the deploy note).
- GATES: lint 0 · tsc 0 · bun test 398/398 · drift guard exit 0 (no schema change) · build:gate PASS · dev server and worker healthy after restore.
- Docs synced: README (full P1-007 LANDED paragraph), deploy doc security note 17 (two-phase rotation runbook incl. crown-jewel handling and ISSUERS guidance), .env.example (FAYANMS_SERVICE_PRIVATE_KEY / FAYANMS_SERVICE_PUBLIC_KEYS with topology comments).

Stage Summary:
- P1-007 LANDED AND E2E-PROVEN: the audit's "holder of the secret can mint any token" is closed at the root — verifiers no longer need minting capability, minters no longer need to share anything, the migration is flag-free and backward-compatible (existing HS256 deployments keep working unchanged), and every failure mode fails tight with a precise code. Phase 2 (removing the shared secret) is now a pure ops decision documented in the deploy runbook; the sandbox stays on the default config until the operator runs the rotation.
- Honest posture: the live sandbox proved Phase 1 (both algs accepted, EdDSA minted and verified in both directions); Phase 2 semantics are pinned by unit tests (SERVICE_ALG_REJECTED/WORKER_ALG_REJECTED) and not forced onto the live sandbox because .env supplies the shared secret by design (documented default state).
- Next executable steps: CERT-006 (Sophos SFOS WebAPI transport) — the last item of the user's ordered SEC/CERT tier.
- Artifacts: this commit (src/lib/auth/service-jwt.ts EdDSA core; src/lib/auth/service-auth.ts mint precedence; mini-services/worker/service-token.ts + control-auth.ts mirrors; scripts/generate-service-keys.ts + keys:service script; tests/auth/service-identity.test.ts; README; deploy note 17; .env.example).

Work Log:
- Read + audited BOTH supplied prompts first. Audit of the prompts: the Phase-1 prompt's "Read first" list is fully satisfiable in-repo EXCEPT AGENTS.md (no root or nested AGENTS.md exists anywhere in the repository — noted honestly rather than invented); the three audit docs it references exist from R34; the mission (TASK-AUTH-001-A) matches the R34 backlog exactly.
