Independently CONFIRMED the AUTH-001 finding before touching code: src/proxy.ts matcher is ["/api/v1/:path*"] (comment says /api/auth/* "never touched") and authorize() in src/lib/auth/options.ts had zero throttling/backoff/lockout/attempt-store (also confirmed LOGIN_FAILED is documented in the schema vocabulary but never emitted).
- Read the actual verification path end-to-end: route.ts (NextAuth catch-all), options.ts (CredentialsProvider authorize + jwt/session callbacks), password.ts (scrypt N=16384), rate-gate.ts (rightmost-trusted-hop resolveClientIp + sliding window), rate-gate tests, AuditEvent schema (actorId NULLABLE — pre-auth telemetry can use the real audit trail without a fabricated FK). Verified next-auth v4 passes authorize(credentials, {query, body, headers, method}) with headers as a plain lowercased record (core/routes/callback.js L328 + next/index.js Object.fromEntries), so the guard reuses resolveClientIp via new Headers(req.headers) — the exact repository trusted-proxy policy, no new IP logic.
- WROTE FAILING TESTS FIRST (prompt §5): tests/audit/login-guard.test.ts — 37 tests across budget/backoff/lockout/decay/reset, trusted-hop keying (leftmost-XFF rotation pin, hops=0 collapse), keyed-HMAC account identity (≠ plain sha256), env clamping, bounded store/stamps, once-per-window telemetry, route pre-check 429 envelope (50-attempt acceptance pin incl. Retry-After + X-Request-Id + no email echo), JSON-body parity, fail-open unparseable bodies, session/signout pass-through, and a real-DB authorize() tier (valid login + USER_LOGIN row, generic null for wrong-password AND unknown account, exact "Account disabled" semantics preserved, throttle-denies-even-valid-credentials pin, SIGNIN_LOCKOUT rows with actorId NULL + keyed resourceLabel), plus structural regression pins (proxy matcher unchanged, guard-before-lookup order in options.ts, route wrapper shape, schema verb documentation).
- Implemented src/lib/auth/login-guard.ts: dedicated abuse-control module (NOT the generic API limiter — different semantics per the prompt). Two enforcement points: (1) route pre-check on POST /api/auth/callback/credentials ONLY (cloned-body parse, fail-open on unparseable bodies, standard 429 envelope reusing rateLimitedBody/rateLimitedHeaders + X-Request-Id) and (2) in-authorize check BEFORE the DB lookup + scrypt with recordLoginFailure/recordLoginSuccess. Keying: resolveClientIp (rightmost-trusted-hop) + HMAC-SHA256(NEXTAUTH_SECRET, normalized email) 128-bit slice — non-reversible, unknown≡known, "unspecified" constant for empty identifiers. Policy: sliding window (FAYANMS_LOGIN_WINDOW_SECONDS, 30–3600 s, default 300), 10 failures/source (FAYANMS_LOGIN_MAX_ATTEMPTS_PER_SOURCE, 3–100), 30 failures/account (FAYANMS_LOGIN_MAX_ATTEMPTS_PER_ACCOUNT, 5–200 — deliberately above the source budget so one client alone cannot lock an account); lockout 30 s·2^n capped at 240 s — kept BELOW the default window so hammering-during-lockout escalation stays coherent; denied attempts on a locked account keep the window hot (escalation), denied attempts on a throttled source consume no slots (truthful Retry-After); full decay + lockoutCount reset when the window slides clean; success deletes both keys. Bounded state: 5,000-key cap with stale-first sweep to half-cap, 64 stamps/key; store behind LoginGuardStore (get/set/delete/size/entries) for SCALE-001-A. Telemetry: SIGNIN_THROTTLED/SIGNIN_LOCKOUT via AuditEvent (actorId NULL, actorName "login-guard", resourceLabel source:/account: keyed hash, ip = sanitized source key), awaited sink wrapped in try/catch (never breaks sign-in), once-per-key-per-window rule (no row storms).
- Wired src/lib/auth/options.ts (guard before lookup — pinned by source-order + runtime behavior; uniform null failures; "Account disabled" throw untouched; USER_LOGIN audit untouched; recordLoginSuccess on success) and route.ts (POST wrapper pre-checks ONLY the credentials callback; GET and every other action delegate to NextAuth untouched; single export pair kept for Next.js route validation).
- Debugging finds (fixed, honestly recorded): (1) next-auth v4 ships provider.authorize as a `() => null` STUB with the real callback under provider.options (core/lib/providers.js parseProviders merges userOptions over the stub) — the test tier extracts it the way the core does; three earlier passes had been vacuous stub-passes, now real. (2) The sandbox shell exports a stale SQLite-era DATABASE_URL (file:...) that overrides .env, and Prisma resolves+caches the URL at client CONSTRUCTION inside the first test file's import graph — a per-file fix was too late when another file built the client first; fixed ONCE per process via bunfig.toml [test] preload = tests/_setup.ts (aligns a missing/file: URL to the documented sandbox PG; NEVER overrides a real postgres:// URL such as CI's). (3) Prisma env-resolution timing verified empirically (lazy at query, cached at construction).
- Gates, in order (prompt §8 + standing loop): focused bun test tests/audit/login-guard.test.ts → 37/37; lint 0; bunx tsc --noEmit FULL 0 (fixed one ProcessEnv/LoginGuardEnv weak-type error); bun test tests/ 456/456 (2,972 expects — 37 new); bunx prisma validate OK; drift guard on a fresh fayanms_shadow: "No difference detected", exit 0 (schema comment change is drift-free); build:gate exit 0. Worker untouched → certify.ts not required (prompt §8 conditional). No test sleeps: injected clocks everywhere in the guard tier; the real-clock authorize tier relies only on thresholds far above execution time.
- Docs synced: README security-posture paragraph (LANDED — AUTH-001-A, honest single-process scope note), deploy-doc security note 20 (full operator runbook semantics), .env.example + docs/deploy/env.production.example (three knobs: units, defaults, clamped ranges, keying, shared-store caveat), docs/audits/FayaNMS-NEXT-TASKS.md (task marked LANDED with the explicit distinction: login abuse control implemented ≠ distributed production rate limiting completed — parent AUTH-001 PARTIALLY FIXED until SCALE-001-A), prisma/schema.prisma action-vocabulary comment (comment-only, drift-free).

- Built tests/e2e/e2e-server.ts: REAL-topology harness — creates/migrates/seeds its own fayanms_e2e database (Bun SQL admin + prisma migrate deploy + the T7 demo seed), boots the PRODUCTION standalone server (.next/standalone/server.js — output:"standalone" makes `next start` mis-serve) and the REAL worker (minimal env modeling the SEC-ENV-001 split — worker-zone material only), readiness-polls /api/v1/meta, tears everything down. FRESH random secrets per run — the deterministic CI fixture values are production-REFUSED by the P1-019 blocklist (exactly as designed).
- Wrote 6 journeys (prompt C1 minimum): J1 auth (login → session/permissions → authorized read → 401 anonymous → CSRF signout → session dead), J2 inventory (201 create → detail → PATCH → 409 duplicate), J3 change SUCCESS path (NORMAL → MEDIUM → two approvals → execute → poll to SUCCESSFUL → re-execute refused) + J3 FAILURE path (STANDARD+rollbackPlan → LOW → TECHNICAL → execute failAt=APPLY → FAILED with truthful per-step states: APPLY FAILED, VALIDATE never succeeded), J4 API client (token-once → bearer ack 200 → narrow-scope 403 → revoke → 401 after), J5 throttle (5×401 → 429 + Retry-After BEFORE scrypt → 30 s recovery → successful login).
- **JOURNEY-FOUND P1 REGRESSION, fixed in the same commit:** POST /api/auth/* 500'd at runtime since R35 — the login-guard wrapper called `handler(req)` WITHOUT the Next route context, so NextAuth destructured `nextauth` from undefined ("Cannot destructure property 'nextauth' from null"). Every runtime credentials sign-in was broken; the unit tier mocked the handler and could not see it. Fix: forward ctx (next-auth v4.24.15 awaits params). This is precisely the class of defect TEST-001 exists to catch — recorded prominently.
- Debug journey (honestly recorded): initial boot refusal (P1-019 fixture secrets + .env FAYANMS_DEMO_MODE leak → fresh secrets + explicit empty override); standalone boot entry; bun 5 s hook/test defaults vs a booting stack and a 31 s recovery wait (explicit timeouts); step assertions match `type` not human `name`; worker env minimized to worker-zone material (the harness now PRACTISES the SEC-ENV-001 split — and its boot warnings had correctly flagged the inherited app-zone vars).

Task ID: R49-preview-db-fix
Agent: Orchestrator (Z.ai Code)
Task: user report "preview view not working check and verify dev server" — diagnose and fix the broken preview (Prisma datasource validation failure on every DB touch), verify the dev server end-to-end.

Work Log:
- Symptom: POST /api/auth/callback/credentials → 401 with `Error validating datasource db: the URL must start with the protocol postgresql://` — the Prisma client was receiving a NON-postgres DATABASE_URL at runtime.
- Root cause chain (empirically proven, process by process): the environment shell exports a stale SQLite-era `DATABASE_URL=file:/home/z/my-project/db/custom.db` (the sandbox /start.sh even writes exactly that value into .env on every boot); `bun run <script>` does NOT inject .env values into the script's child environment (probe-verified in an isolated dir), so the polluted shell value flowed bun → bash → next dev → next-server; Next.js env loading never overrides an already-present variable, so the correct `postgresql://fayanms:fayanms@localhost:5433/fayanms` in .env was shadowed; Prisma (provider postgresql) rejected the `file:` URL on first use → every DB-touching request failed → sign-in 401 → "preview not working". bunfig.toml's comment confirms the phenomenon was already known and neutralized for TESTS (tests/_setup.ts preload) — nothing did it for the dev server.
- Fix 1 (package.json dev script): `case` guard mirroring tests/_setup.ts semantics exactly — a missing or `file:`-shaped DATABASE_URL is replaced with the documented embedded-PG URL; a real `postgres(ql)://` value (CI, operator) is always respected. No credential material added to the repo (the URL is the documented .env/.env.example dev default).
- Fix 2 (src/instrumentation.ts): a second defect surfaced during diagnosis — the edge bundle of the instrumentation hook statically pulled security-policy → service-jwt → node:crypto into the edge sandbox (Turbopack "Ecmascript file had an error" on every dev boot). register() now gates the dynamic import on `NEXT_RUNTIME === "nodejs"` (statically replaced per runtime, edge build eliminates the branch). Fresh boots are clean of the edge error.
- Ops: killed stale dev-server processes (incl. an old supervisor bun that re-spawned the chain with the polluted env), restarted via the hardened script, verified in-call that next dev's exec env now carries the postgres URL. Server processes spawned from tool shells die at tool-call boundaries in the current sandbox; the boot flow (.zscripts/dev.sh → bun run dev) uses the SAME hardened script from the init tree, so the user-facing preview self-heals at the next boot/restart.
- Data: the embedded PG cluster was reachable but the User table was EMPTY (boot flow runs db:push only — no seed), which alone 401s every login; ran `bun prisma/seed.ts` (canonical reset-style demo bootstrap, 66,108 rows incl. the demo users) against the documented URL.
- Golden-path verification (agent-browser, real seeded credentials admin@faya.local — NOT a fake login): sign-in → authenticated AppShell rendered (sidebar nav, palette, notifications, dashboard KPIs: 29 managed devices, 25 online, 1 critical alert, 4 incidents, 93.1% backup compliance), Prisma queries flowing against PostgreSQL (`/api/v1/devices 200`), ZERO console/page errors. Screenshots: /tmp/fayanms-dashboard.png.
- README sync: Scripts-table `bun run dev` row documents the self-alignment semantics; R49 LANDED block added with the full root-cause narrative.
